## Inputs and outputs — the box

From the BIM part [`rigol/ds1104z`](/api/bim/parts?full=1&id=rigol/ds1104z) — DS1000Z series user guide, front and rear panel — declared, not measured

### Inputs

| Terminal | Connector | Domain | Count | Signal |
|---|---|---|---:|---|
| CH | COAX-BNC | signal | 4 | analog channel, 1 MΩ |
| EXT TRIG | COAX-BNC | signal | 1 | external trigger |

### Outputs

| Terminal | Connector | Domain | Count | Signal |
|---|---|---|---:|---|
| AUX OUT | COAX-BNC | signal | 1 | trigger out or pass/fail, whichever the menu selects |

### Connections (control and network)

| Terminal | Connector | Domain | Count | Signal |
|---|---|---|---:|---|
| LAN | RJ45 | network | 1 | SCPI over LAN |
| USB DEVICE | USB-B | control | 1 | USBTMC — the bus this bench reaches it on |
| USB HOST | USB-A | control | 1 | storage and printer |

**Power:** MAINS IN (IEC-C13)

<!-- /sdk:io -->

## Driving it

Three doors, all on the bench's own services — none of them is a per-model process.

1. **The bus, through KOMAND** (what a panel and the touch pad do). Teach the topic its handler, then send the value; never retained — KOMAND ignores a retained value on purpose. `target` is the box's Write topic (`GET /api/instrument/status?protocol=visa&resource=…` → `write_topic`); a DO/NAB fires on a truthy value.

```sh
T=APK.audio/Gui/MyPanel/Set_Ese
mosquitto_pub -t "$T/config" -m '{"komand_handler": {"enable": true, "komand_type": "set", "command": "Set_Ese", "model": "DS1104Z", "input_name": "value", "target": "<box>/Write"}}'
mosquitto_pub -t "$T" -m '{"value": <value>}'
```

2. **Raw SCPI** — `POST /api/instrument/send` with `{"protocol": "visa", "resource": "<VISA resource>", "command": "<SCPI>"}`; the answer comes back in the reply and on `<box>/Read`.

3. **The spec, over HTTP** — `GET /api/instrument/Oscilloscope/DS1104Z` (this model's whole `api.json`), `GET /api/instrument/Oscilloscope/DS1104Z/<verb>/<command>` (one command's entry), `GET …/health`. These describe; they do not send anything to the box.

A read's answer is published on `<box>/Reading/<command>` (the **Out** column below); `<box>` is `APK.audio/System/Protocols/visa/Device/<Family>/<Model>/<Dev…>`.
